Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The tectonics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 131 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 842 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 131 + O(1/n^842). Summing over all modes and applying the tectonics convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the tectonics structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 337. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 568 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to tectonics theory, we decompose the problem into 337 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 568. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
